```cpp
                        &B_packed.packed_scales_bf16[(p * num_groups + g) * kNr8];
                    float scale_f32[kNr8];
                    for (size_t c = 0; c < kNr8; ++c) {
                        scale_f32[c] = bf16_bits_to_fp32(scale_bf16_ptr[c]);
                    }
                    for (size_t k = k_base; k < k_end; ++k) {
                        const uint16_t word = trits_ptr[k];
                        for (size_t r = 0; r < m_valid; ++r) {
                            const float a_val = A(static_cast<int64_t>(ir + r), static_cast<int64_t>(k));
                            for (size_t c = 0; c < n_valid; ++c) {
                                const int code = static_cast<int>((word >> (2 * c)) & 0x3u);
                                const float w_val = static_cast<float>(code - 1) * scale_f32[c];
                                tmp[r][c] += a_val * w_val;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                for (size_t r = 0; r < m_valid; ++r) {
                    for (size_t c = 0; c < n_valid; ++c) {
                        C(static_cast<int64_t>(ir + r),
                          static_cast<int64_t>(n_base + c)) = tmp[r][c];
                    }
                }
#endif
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * @brief PowerInfer-style offline placement: pre-populates Tier-1 VRAM with the highest-scoring panels
     *        (e.g. calibration activation frequency x weight norm) so the first tokens avoid compulsory
     *        misses. Resets residency first. Ties are broken by lower panel index (deterministic).
     */
    void seed_offline_powerlaw_hot_panels(const std::vector<float>& panel_importance_scores) {
        const size_t n = std::min(panel_importance_scores.size(), panel_in_vram_.size());
        std::fill(panel_in_vram_.begin(), panel_in_vram_.end(), false);
        current_vram_panels_ = 0;
        std::vector<size_t> order(n);
        std::iota(order.begin(), order.end(), size_t{0});
        const size_t take = std::min(max_vram_panels_, n);
        std::partial_sort(order.begin(), order.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(take), order.end(),
                          [&](size_t a, size_t b) {
                              return panel_importance_scores[a] > panel_importance_scores[b] ||
                                     (panel_importance_scores[a] == panel_importance_scores[b] && a < b);
                          });
        for (size_t i = 0; i < take; ++i) {
            panel_in_vram_[order[i]] = true;
            // Give seeded panels the promotion threshold so a single cold access cannot evict them
            panel_access_counts_[order[i]] = std::max(panel_access_counts_[order[i]], promotion_threshold_);
            ++current_vram_panels_;
        }
    }

    /**
     * @brief PowerInfer-2-style windowed aging: halves every access counter so a change of topic can evict
     *        stale panels instead of letting counters saturate. Call every W tokens.
     */
    void decay_access_frequencies() noexcept {
        for (auto& c : panel_access_counts_) c >>= 1;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool panel_resident_in_vram(size_t p) const noexcept {
        return p < panel_in_vram_.size() && panel_in_vram_[p];
    }

    /**
     * @brief Allocates a 64B-aligned KV-cache page from the prefaulted SlabAllocator in O(1) time.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] void* allocate_kv_page() {
        ++telemetry_.kv_pages_allocated;
        return kv_page_slab_.allocate();
    }

    void free_kv_page(void* page_ptr) noexcept {
        kv_page_slab_.deallocate(page_ptr);
    }

    void record_kv_page_centroid_filter(size_t total_pages, size_t skipped_pages) noexcept {
        (void)total_pages;
        telemetry_.kv_pages_skipped_by_centroid += skipped_pages;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const SmartCacheTelemetry& telemetry() const noexcept { return telemetry_; }

private:
    // Per-group L1 activation energy: summed over rows (legacy tau heuristic) and max over rows (error bound).
    // Non-finite row energy is mapped to +inf so the bound never admits skipping it and sorting stays well-defined.
    void compute_group_energies(const TensorView<const float>& A, size_t K, size_t num_groups) {
        const size_t M = static_cast<size_t>(A.size(0));
        group_energy_sum_.assign(num_groups, 0.0f);
        group_row_max_l1_.assign(num_groups, 0.0f);
        float total = 0.0f;
        for (size_t g = 0; g < num_groups; ++g) {
            const size_t k_base = g * kGroupSizeG128;
            const size_t k_end = std::min(K, k_base + kGroupSizeG128);
            float sum = 0.0f, row_max = 0.0f;
            for (size_t m = 0; m < M; ++m) {
                float row_l1 = 0.0f;
                for (size_t k = k_base; k < k_end; ++k) {
                    row_l1 += std::fabs(A(static_cast<int64_t>(m), static_cast<int64_t>(k)));
                }
                if (!std::isfinite(row_l1)) row_l1 = std::numeric_limits<float>::infinity();
                sum += row_l1;
                row_max = std::max(row_max, row_l1);
            }
            group_energy_sum_[g] = sum;
            group_row_max_l1_[g] = row_max;
            total += sum;
        }
        mean_energy_ = (num_groups > 0) ? total / static_cast<float>(num_groups) : 0.0f;
    }

    // Fills skip_[g] for panel p according to the active sieve policy (see class comment).
    void plan_panel_sieve(const std::vector<uint16_t>& scales_bf16, size_t p, size_t num_groups,
                          size_t n_valid, bool force_exact) {
        skip_.assign(num_groups, 0);
        if (force_exact) return;
        if (error_budget_ > 0.0f) {
            bound_.resize(num_groups);
            order_.resize(num_groups);
            for (size_t g = 0; g < num_groups; ++g) {
                float smax = 0.0f;
                for (size_t c = 0; c < n_valid; ++c) {
                    smax = std::max(smax, std::fabs(bf16_bits_to_fp32(scales_bf16[(p * num_groups + g) * kNr8 + c])));
                }
                const float e = group_row_max_l1_[g];
                bound_[g] = std::isinf(e) ? e : smax * e;
                if (!std::isfinite(bound_[g])) bound_[g] = std::numeric_limits<float>::infinity();
            }
            std::iota(order_.begin(), order_.end(), size_t{0});
            std::stable_sort(order_.begin(), order_.end(), [&](size_t x, size_t y) { return bound_[x] < bound_[y]; });
            double spent = 0.0;
            for (const size_t g : order_) {
                if (spent + static_cast<double>(bound_[g]) > static_cast<double>(error_budget_)) break;
                spent += static_cast<double>(bound_[g]);
                skip_[g] = 1;
            }
        } else if (tau_ > 0.0f) {
            const float threshold = tau_ * mean_energy_;
            for (size_t g = 0; g < num_groups; ++g) skip_[g] = (group_energy_sum_[g] < threshold) ? 1 : 0;
        }
    }

private:
    size_t                max_vram_panels_{0};
    size_t                current_vram_panels_{0};
    uint64_t              access_clock_{0};
    float                 tau_{0.0f};
    float                 error_budget_{0.0f};
    uint32_t              promotion_threshold_{2};
    std::vector<uint32_t> panel_access_counts_;
    std::vector<uint64_t> panel_last_touch_;
    std::vector<bool>     panel_in_vram_;
    SlabAllocator<UHA_KV_PAGE_INT4_BYTES> kv_page_slab_;
    SmartCacheTelemetry   telemetry_{};
    // Scratch reused across GEMM calls (not thread-safe: one cache instance per thread)
    std::vector<float>    group_energy_sum_;
    std::vector<float>    group_row_max_l1_;
    std::vector<float>    bound_;
    std::vector<size_t>   order_;
    std::vector<uint8_t>  skip_;
    float                 mean_energy_{0.0f};
};

} // namespace kinesis::unified
```

## File: `kinesis_unified/test_kinesis_unified.cpp`
```cpp
// File: kinesis_unified/test_kinesis_unified.cpp
// Standalone C++20 verification & benchmark suite for the unified Kinesis + Sub-2-Bit Smart Cache engine.
// Build: g++ -O3 -std=c++20 -mavx2 -mfma -o test_kinesis_unified test_kinesis_unified.cpp

#include "memory_primitives.hpp"
#include "hardware_and_sandbox.hpp"
#include "sub2bit_gemm_and_smart_cache.hpp"

#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <vector>

#define UHA_VERIFY(cond)                                                                       \
    do {                                                                                       \
        if (!(cond)) {                                                                         \
            std::fprintf(stderr, "[FATAL] Verification failed at %s:%d: %s\n",                 \
                         __FILE__, __LINE__, #cond);                                           \
            std::abort();                                                                      \
        }                                                                                      \
    } while (0)

using namespace kinesis::unified;

int main() {
    std::printf("================================================================================\n");
    std::printf("  WORKSTREAM 9: KINESIS UNIFIED C++20 CORE, AVX2 SUB-2-BIT & SMART CACHE SUITE\n");
    std::printf("================================================================================\n\n");

    // 1. Verify Live CPUID Hardware Discovery & Analytical BLIS Cache Blocking
    const CpuFeatures cpu = HardwareInfo::query_cpu_features();
    const BlisCacheBlocks blk = HardwareInfo::compute_blis_blocks(cpu);
    std::printf("[1] Live CPUID Topology & Analytical BLIS Cache Blocking:\n");
    std::printf("    Vendor = %s | Cores = %u | AVX2 = %d | FMA = %d | AVX512F/BW = %d/%d | AMX = %d\n",
                cpu.vendor.c_str(), cpu.logical_cores, cpu.has_avx2 ? 1 : 0,
                cpu.has_fma ? 1 : 0, cpu.has_avx512f ? 1 : 0, cpu.has_avx512bw ? 1 : 0,
                cpu.has_amx_bf16 ? 1 : 0);
    std::printf("    L1D = %zu KB | L2 = %zu KB | L3 = %zu KB\n",
                cpu.l1d_cache_bytes / 1024, cpu.l2_cache_bytes / 1024, cpu.l3_cache_bytes / 1024);
    std::printf("    Derived BLIS Tiles: MR = %zu, NR = %zu, KC = %zu, MC = %zu, NC = %zu -> PASS\n\n",
                blk.mr, blk.nr, blk.kc, blk.mc, blk.nc);
    UHA_VERIFY(cpu.l1d_cache_bytes > 0 && blk.kc >= 64);

    // 2. Verify Three-Lifetime Prefaulted & Page-Locked Arena + SlabAllocator
    WorkspacePlan plan{
        4 * 1024 * 1024, // 4 MB Persistent weight arena
        2 * 1024 * 1024, // 2 MB Step activation arena
        4 * 1024 * 1024  // 4 MB Transient scratch arena
    };
    ThreeTierArenaManager arenas(plan);
    void* p_weight = arenas.persistent().allocate(1024, 64);
    void* p_step   = arenas.step().allocate(2048, 64);
    UHA_VERIFY(is_aligned(p_weight, 64));
    UHA_VERIFY(is_aligned(p_step, 64));
    UHA_VERIFY(arenas.persistent().owns(p_weight));
    UHA_VERIFY(arenas.step().owns(p_step));
    UHA_VERIFY(!arenas.persistent().owns(p_step));

    SlabAllocator<256, 64> slab(128, true);
    void* b0 = slab.allocate(256);
    void* b1 = slab.allocate(256);
    UHA_VERIFY(slab.allocated_blocks() == 2);
    slab.deallocate(b1);
    slab.deallocate(b0);
    UHA_VERIFY(slab.free_blocks() == 128);
    std::printf("[2] Prefaulted & Page-Locked Three-Lifetime Arenas + O(1) SlabAllocator:\n");
    std::printf("    Persistent = %zu KB (Locked=%d), Step = %zu KB, Scratch = %zu KB, Slab Free = %zu/128 -> PASS\n\n",
                arenas.persistent().capacity() / 1024,
                arenas.persistent().is_page_locked() ? 1 : 0,
                arenas.step().capacity() / 1024,
                arenas.scratch().capacity() / 1024,
                slab.free_blocks());

    // 3. Verify MemorySandbox: CPU/GPU Cycle Separation & 1.00x Conflict-Free 32-Bank Swizzle
    const WarpBankReport unswizzled = MemorySandbox::evaluate_warp_32bank_access(128, false);
    const WarpBankReport swizzled   = MemorySandbox::evaluate_warp_32bank_access(128, true);
    const WarpBankReport ldm_unswiz = MemorySandbox::evaluate_ldmatrix_8x16b_bank_access(128, false);
    const WarpBankReport ldm_swiz   = MemorySandbox::evaluate_ldmatrix_8x16b_bank_access(128, true);
    UHA_VERIFY(unswizzled.max_way_conflict == 32);
    UHA_VERIFY(swizzled.max_way_conflict == 1 && swizzled.active_banks == 32);
    UHA_VERIFY(ldm_unswiz.max_way_conflict == 8);
    UHA_VERIFY(ldm_swiz.max_way_conflict == 1 && ldm_swiz.active_banks == 32);

    MemorySandbox sandbox(cpu.l1d_cache_bytes, 64, 8);
    // Simulate streaming 4x8 packed micro-panel hits in L1 cache
    for (size_t pass = 0; pass < 8; ++pass) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < 256; ++i) {
            sandbox.record_access(0x100000ULL + i * 32ULL, 32);
        }
    }
    const CacheSandboxReport& srep = sandbox.report();
    UHA_VERIFY(srep.hit_rate_percent() > 90.0);
    std::printf("[3] MemorySandbox Verification (Fixes Design Review L5 & CPU/GPU Latency Mixup):\n");
    std::printf("    Scalar 32-Lane Bank Conflict : Unswizzled = %u-way, Swizzled = %u-way (%u/32 banks)\n",
                unswizzled.max_way_conflict, swizzled.max_way_conflict, swizzled.active_banks);
    std::printf("    ldmatrix 8x16B Bank Conflict : Unswizzled = %u-way, Swizzled = %u-way (%u/32 banks)\n",
                ldm_unswiz.max_way_conflict, ldm_swiz.max_way_conflict, ldm_swiz.active_banks);
    std::printf("    Packed Panel L1 Hit Rate = %.2f%% | CPU Cycles = %zu | GPU Cycles = %zu -> PASS\n\n",
                srep.hit_rate_percent(), srep.cpu_estimated_cycles, srep.gpu_estimated_cycles);

    // 4. Verify True 4x8 Register-Blocked FP32 GotoBLAS GEMM (Zero Heap Allocations)
    const size_t M = 64, K = 256, N = 64;
    auto* A_buf = static_cast<float*>(arenas.step().allocate(M * K * sizeof(float), 64));
    auto* B_buf = static_cast<float*>(arenas.persistent().allocate(K * N * sizeof(float), 64));
    auto* C_buf = static_cast<float*>(arenas.step().allocate(M * N * sizeof(float), 64));
    auto* C_ref = static_cast<float*>(arenas.step().allocate(M * N * sizeof(float), 64));

    for (size_t i = 0; i < M * K; ++i) A_buf[i] = static_cast<float>((static_cast<int>(i % 17) - 8)) * 0.125f;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < K * N; ++i) B_buf[i] = static_cast<float>((static_cast<int>(i % 13) - 6)) * 0.25f;

    TensorView<const float> A_view(A_buf, Shape{static_cast<int64_t>(M), static_cast<int64_t>(K)});
    TensorView<const float> B_view(B_buf, Shape{static_cast<int64_t>(K), static_cast<int64_t>(N)});
    TensorView<float>       C_view(C_buf, Shape{static_cast<int64_t>(M), static_cast<int64_t>(N)});

    gemm_fp32_register_blocked_4x8(A_view, B_view, C_view, arenas.scratch());

    float max_fp32_err = 0.0f;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < M; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            float acc = 0.0f;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < K; ++k) {
                acc += A_view(static_cast<int64_t>(i), static_cast<int64_t>(k)) *
                       B_view(static_cast<int64_t>(k), static_cast<int64_t>(j));
            }
            C_ref[i * N + j] = acc;
            const float diff = std::fabs(C_view(static_cast<int64_t>(i), static_cast<int64_t>(j)) - acc);
            if (diff > max_fp32_err) max_fp32_err = diff;
        }
    }
    UHA_VERIFY(max_fp32_err < 1e-5f);
    std::printf("[4] 4x8 Register-Blocked FP32 GotoBLAS GEMM (%zux%zux%zu, Scratch Arena Only):\n", M, K, N);
    std::printf("    Max Numerical Error vs Reference = %.2e (Scratch Reset Offset = %zu) -> PASS\n\n",
                max_fp32_err, arenas.scratch().allocated_bytes());

    // 5. Verify Sub-2-Bit (1.125 bpw) GotoBLAS Panel Packing & Branchless AVX2 Unpacker
    const W1GotoBlasPanelSet w1_panels = pack_w1_gotoblas_panels(B_view);
    const double comp_ratio = w1_panels.compression_ratio_vs_fp32();
    UHA_VERIFY(comp_ratio > 28.0); // 32 bits / 1.125 bits = 28.44x compression vs FP32!

    SmartTieredCache smart_cache(w1_panels.num_panels, w1_panels.num_panels / 2, 0.90f, 1);
    TensorView<float> C_w1_exact(C_buf, Shape{static_cast<int64_t>(M), static_cast<int64_t>(N)});
    smart_cache.execute_smart_w1_gemm(A_view, w1_panels, C_w1_exact, /*force_exact=*/true);

    // Compute exact dequantized W1 reference and verify zero mismatch with AVX2 branchless unpacker
    float max_w1_unpack_err = 0.0f;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < M; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            const size_t p = j / kNr8;
            const size_t c = j % kNr8;
            float acc = 0.0f;
            for (size_t g = 0; g < w1_panels.groups_per_col; ++g) {
                const float scale = bf16_bits_to_fp32(
                    w1_panels.packed_scales_bf16[(p * w1_panels.groups_per_col + g) * kNr8 + c]
                );
                const size_t k_base = g * kGroupSizeG128;
                const size_t k_end = std::min(K, k_base + kGroupSizeG128);
                for (size_t k = k_base; k < k_end; ++k) {
                    const uint8_t bmask = w1_panels.packed_bits[p * K + k];
                    const float w_val = ((bmask >> c) & 1u) ? scale : -scale;
                    acc += A_view(static_cast<int64_t>(i), static_cast<int64_t>(k)) * w_val;
                }
            }
            const float diff = std::fabs(C_w1_exact(static_cast<int64_t>(i), static_cast<int64_t>(j)) - acc);
            if (diff > max_w1_unpack_err) max_w1_unpack_err = diff;
        }
    }
    UHA_VERIFY(max_w1_unpack_err < 1e-5f);

    // Verify W2 (2.125 bpw Ternary) GotoBLAS Panel Packing (15.06x vs FP32) & AVX2 Unpacker
    const W2GotoBlasPanelSet w2_panels = pack_w2_gotoblas_panels(B_view);
    const double w2_comp_ratio = w2_panels.compression_ratio_vs_fp32();
    UHA_VERIFY(w2_comp_ratio > 15.0); // 32 bits / 2.125 bits = 15.06x compression vs FP32

    TensorView<float> C_w2_exact(C_buf, Shape{static_cast<int64_t>(M), static_cast<int64_t>(N)});
    smart_cache.execute_smart_w2_gemm(A_view, w2_panels, C_w2_exact, /*force_exact=*/true);
    float max_w2_unpack_err = 0.0f;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < M; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            const size_t p = j / kNr8;
            const size_t c = j % kNr8;
            float acc = 0.0f;
            for (size_t g = 0; g < w2_panels.groups_per_col; ++g) {
                const float scale = bf16_bits_to_fp32(
                    w2_panels.packed_scales_bf16[(p * w2_panels.groups_per_col + g) * kNr8 + c]
                );
                const size_t k_base = g * kGroupSizeG128;
                const size_t k_end = std::min(K, k_base + kGroupSizeG128);
                for (size_t k = k_base; k < k_end; ++k) {
                    const uint16_t word = w2_panels.packed_trits[p * K + k];
                    const int code = static_cast<int>((word >> (2 * c)) & 0x3u);
                    const float w_val = static_cast<float>(code - 1) * scale;
                    acc += A_view(static_cast<int64_t>(i), static_cast<int64_t>(k)) * w_val;
                }
            }
            const float diff = std::fabs(C_w2_exact(static_cast<int64_t>(i), static_cast<int64_t>(j)) - acc);
            if (diff > max_w2_unpack_err) max_w2_unpack_err = diff;
        }
    }
    UHA_VERIFY(max_w2_unpack_err < 1e-5f);

    // Verify lossless CPU GotoBLAS Nr=8 <-> GPU Interleaved 16-Bit Split uint32 Layout Bridge
    std::vector<uint32_t> gpu_w_u32;
    std::vector<uint16_t> gpu_scales_bf16;
    convert_w1_gotoblas_to_gpu_interleaved_u32(w1_panels, gpu_w_u32, gpu_scales_bf16);
    UHA_VERIFY(gpu_w_u32.size() == N * (K / UHA_W1_WEIGHTS_PER_U32));
    size_t layout_bit_mismatches = 0;
    for (size_t n = 0; n < N; ++n) {
        const size_t p = n / kNr8;
        const size_t c = n % kNr8;
        for (size_t k = 0; k < K; ++k) {
            const uint32_t cpu_bit = (w1_panels.packed_bits[p * K + k] >> c) & 1U;
            const size_t w_idx = k / 32;
            const size_t pair_idx = (k % 32) / 2;
            const size_t is_odd = k & 1U;
            const uint32_t gpu_word = gpu_w_u32[n * (K / 32) + w_idx];
            const uint32_t gpu_bit = (gpu_word >> (pair_idx + is_odd * 16)) & 1U;
            if (cpu_bit != gpu_bit) ++layout_bit_mismatches;
        }
    }
    UHA_VERIFY(layout_bit_mismatches == 0);

    std::printf("[5] Sub-2-Bit GotoBLAS Panel Packers (W1 1.125 bpw & W2 2.125 bpw) + GPU Layout Bridge:\n");
    std::printf("    Descriptor Sizes: Shape = %zu B, Strides = %zu B, TensorView = %zu B (2x64B Cache Lines)\n",
                sizeof(Shape), sizeof(Strides), sizeof(TensorView<float>));
    std::printf("    W1 (1.125 bpw): %zu B -> %zu B (%.2fx vs FP32, %.2fx vs FP16) | AVX2 Err = %.2e\n",
                w1_panels.fp32_equivalent_bytes(), w1_panels.compressed_bytes(),
                comp_ratio, comp_ratio / 2.0, max_w1_unpack_err);
    std::printf("    W2 (2.125 bpw): %zu B -> %zu B (%.2fx vs FP32, %.2fx vs FP16) | AVX2 Err = %.2e | GPU Bridge Mismatches = %zu -> PASS\n\n",
                w2_panels.fp32_equivalent_bytes(), w2_panels.compressed_bytes(),
                w2_comp_ratio, w2_comp_ratio / 2.0, max_w2_unpack_err, layout_bit_mismatches);

    // 6. Verify Smart CPU + Single-GPU Tiered Cache with Predictive Tiled-CBC Energy Sieve & LFU Eviction
    for (size_t m = 0; m < M; ++m) {
        for (size_t k = 0; k < 128; ++k) {
            A_buf[m * K + k] *= 0.05f;
        }
    }
    SmartTieredCache tiered_cache(w1_panels.num_panels, /*max_vram_hot_panels=*/4, /*tau=*/0.85f, /*promo=*/1);
    // Pass 1: Cold panels computed in CPU L3 via AVX2 and first 4 panels promoted to Tier-1 VRAM
    tiered_cache.execute_smart_w1_gemm(A_view, w1_panels, C_w1_exact, /*force_exact=*/false);
    // Touch panel 5 repeatedly so it out-heats resident panel 0 and triggers LFU Tier-1 VRAM eviction
    tiered_cache.record_panel_access_and_promote(5);
    tiered_cache.record_panel_access_and_promote(5);
    // Pass 2: Promoted panels now hit directly in Tier-1 Single-GPU VRAM cache
    tiered_cache.execute_smart_w1_gemm(A_view, w1_panels, C_w1_exact, /*force_exact=*/false);

    void* kv_page = tiered_cache.allocate_kv_page();
    UHA_VERIFY(kv_page != nullptr);
    tiered_cache.free_kv_page(kv_page);

    const SmartCacheTelemetry& tel = tiered_cache.telemetry();
    UHA_VERIFY(tel.tiles_pruned_by_energy_sieve > 0);
    UHA_VERIFY(tel.hot_tile_promotions >= 5);
    UHA_VERIFY(tel.tier1_vram_evictions >= 1);
    UHA_VERIFY(tel.tier1_vram_hits > 0);
    std::printf("[6] Smart CPU-L3 + Single-GPU VRAM Tiered Cache & Tiled-CBC Predictive Sieve:\n");
    std::printf("    Total Tile Requests = %zu | Pruned by Energy Sieve = %zu (%.1f%% Skipped Before Fetch)\n",
                tel.total_tile_requests, tel.tiles_pruned_by_energy_sieve, tel.pruning_ratio_percent());
    std::printf("    Tier-2 CPU L3 Hits = %zu | Promotions = %zu | Evictions = %zu | Tier-1 VRAM Hits = %zu\n",
                tel.tier2_cpu_l3_hits, tel.hot_tile_promotions, tel.tier1_vram_evictions, tel.tier1_vram_hits);
    std::printf("    Modelled weight-traffic ratio (compression x 1/(1 - skipped), NOT measured) = %.2fx (vs FP32) / %.2fx (vs FP16) -> PASS\n",
                tel.effective_bandwidth_multiplier(comp_ratio),
                tel.effective_bandwidth_multiplier(comp_ratio / 2.0));

    // 7. T-MAC style 16-entry LUT kernel (decode regime, M <= 8) + zero-group fix + PowerInfer seeding/aging
    {
        const size_t Ml = 4;
        TensorView<const float> A_small(A_buf, Shape{static_cast<int64_t>(Ml), static_cast<int64_t>(K)});
        const W1TmacLutPanelSet lut = pack_w1_tmac_lut_panels(w1_panels);
        UHA_VERIFY(lut.nibbles.size() == w1_panels.num_panels * (K / 4));

        std::vector<float> c_avx(Ml * N), c_scalar(Ml * N);
        TensorView<float> v_avx(c_avx.data(), Shape{static_cast<int64_t>(Ml), static_cast<int64_t>(N)});
        TensorView<float> v_sc(c_scalar.data(), Shape{static_cast<int64_t>(Ml), static_cast<int64_t>(N)});
        execute_tmac_lut_w1_gemm(A_small, lut, v_avx, 0.0f, /*force_scalar=*/false);
        execute_tmac_lut_w1_gemm(A_small, lut, v_sc, 0.0f, /*force_scalar=*/true);

        // (a) SIMD pshufb path and portable path use identical integer arithmetic: must match exactly
        for (size_t i = 0; i < Ml * N; ++i) UHA_VERIFY(c_avx[i] == c_scalar[i]);

        // (b) Accuracy vs exact FP32 W1 reference stays inside the analytic int8-activation rounding bound
        float worst_ratio = 0.0f, max_lut_err = 0.0f;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < Ml; ++i) {
            for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
                const size_t p = j / kNr8, c = j % kNr8;
                float ref = 0.0f, bound = 0.0f;
                for (size_t g = 0; g < w1_panels.groups_per_col; ++g) {
                    const float sc_w = bf16_bits_to_fp32(
                        w1_panels.packed_scales_bf16[(p * w1_panels.groups_per_col + g) * kNr8 + c]);
                    float amax = 0.0f;
                    for (size_t k = g * kGroupSizeG128; k < (g + 1) * kGroupSizeG128; ++k) {
                        const float a = A_small(static_cast<int64_t>(i), static_cast<int64_t>(k));
                        amax = std::max(amax, std::fabs(a));
                        const float w = ((w1_panels.packed_bits[p * K + k] >> c) & 1u) ? sc_w : -sc_w;
                        ref += a * w;
                    }
                    bound += 0.5f * (amax / 127.0f) * 128.0f * sc_w;
                }
                const float err = std::fabs(c_avx[i * N + j] - ref);
                max_lut_err = std::max(max_lut_err, err);
                worst_ratio = std::max(worst_ratio, err / (bound + 1e-6f));
            }
        }
        UHA_VERIFY(worst_ratio <= 1.001f);

        // (c) An all-zero weight group must give exactly zero output (regression: scale used to be forced to 1.0)
        std::vector<float> zeros(128 * 8, 0.0f), a_ones(1 * 128, 1.0f), c_zero(8, -1.0f);
        TensorView<const float> Wz(zeros.data(), Shape{128, 8});
        TensorView<const float> Az(a_ones.data(), Shape{1, 128});
        TensorView<float> Cz(c_zero.data(), Shape{1, 8});
        const W1GotoBlasPanelSet wz = pack_w1_gotoblas_panels(Wz);
        SmartTieredCache zero_cache(wz.num_panels, 0, 0.0f, 1);
        zero_cache.execute_smart_w1_gemm(Az, wz, Cz, /*force_exact=*/true);
        for (size_t j = 0; j < 8; ++j) UHA_VERIFY(c_zero[j] == 0.0f);

        // (d) Offline power-law seeding places exactly the top-scoring panels; aging halves counters
        SmartTieredCache seeded(8, 3, 0.85f, 2);
        seeded.seed_offline_powerlaw_hot_panels({0.1f, 9.0f, 0.2f, 8.0f, 0.3f, 7.0f, 0.4f, 0.5f});
        UHA_VERIFY(seeded.panel_resident_in_vram(1) && seeded.panel_resident_in_vram(3) &&
                   seeded.panel_resident_in_vram(5) && !seeded.panel_resident_in_vram(0));
        seeded.record_panel_access_and_promote(1);
        UHA_VERIFY(seeded.telemetry().tier1_vram_hits == 1);
        seeded.decay_access_frequencies();

        // Sieve path must skip some (row, group) pairs when one group is nearly silent
        std::vector<float> a_skew(1 * K, 1.0f), c_skew(N, 0.0f);
        for (size_t k = 0; k < 128; ++k) a_skew[k] = 0.001f;
        TensorView<const float> A_sk(a_skew.data(), Shape{1, static_cast<int64_t>(K)});
        TensorView<float> C_sk(c_skew.data(), Shape{1, static_cast<int64_t>(N)});
        const TmacRunStats st = execute_tmac_lut_w1_gemm(A_sk, lut, C_sk, 0.85f, false);
        UHA_VERIFY(st.groups_skipped == 1 && st.groups_total == lut.groups_per_col);

        std::printf("[7] T-MAC 16-entry LUT kernel (M=%zu), zero-group fix, offline seeding:\n", Ml);
        std::printf("    pshufb vs scalar LUT mismatches = 0 | max |err| vs FP32 W1 = %.3e (%.1f%% of analytic bound) | zero-group exact = PASS\n",
                    max_lut_err, 100.0 * worst_ratio);
        std::printf("    NOTE: accuracy only; no speedup is claimed or measured for this kernel -> PASS\n");
    }

    // 8. Sieve policies: exact by default, guaranteed error budget, and a negative control for the
    //    legacy relative-energy heuristic (docs/11 O8, O9)
    {
        const size_t Ms = 8, Ks = 1024, Ns = 24; // 8 K-groups, 3 panels
        uint32_t lcg = 12345U;
        auto rnd = [&lcg]() {
            lcg = lcg * 1664525U + 1013904223U;
            return static_cast<float>((lcg >> 8) & 0xFFFFU) / 32768.0f - 1.0f; // [-1, 1)
        };
        std::vector<float> w_s(Ks * Ns), a_s(Ms * Ks);
        for (auto& v : w_s) v = rnd();
        for (size_t m = 0; m < Ms; ++m) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < Ks; ++k) {
                const size_t g = k / kGroupSizeG128;
                // Groups 0..3 are quiet for every row; group 1 has ONE loud row (row 5): the case where the
                // summed-over-rows heuristic drops a group that matters to a single row.
                float amp = (g < 4) ? 0.0005f : 1.0f;
                if (g == 1 && m == 5) amp = 0.5f;
                a_s[m * Ks + k] = amp * rnd();
            }
        }
        TensorView<const float> Ws(w_s.data(), Shape{static_cast<int64_t>(Ks), static_cast<int64_t>(Ns)});
        TensorView<const float> As(a_s.data(), Shape{static_cast<int64_t>(Ms), static_cast<int64_t>(Ks)});
        const W1GotoBlasPanelSet w1s = pack_w1_gotoblas_panels(Ws);
        const W2GotoBlasPanelSet w2s = pack_w2_gotoblas_panels(Ws);

        std::vector<float> c_ref(Ms * Ns), c_def(Ms * Ns), c_bud(Ms * Ns), c_tau(Ms * Ns);
        auto view = [&](std::vector<float>& v) {
            return TensorView<float>(v.data(), Shape{static_cast<int64_t>(Ms), static_cast<int64_t>(Ns)});
        };
        auto max_abs_diff = [&](const std::vector<float>& x, const std::vector<float>& y) {
            float d = 0.0f;
            for (size_t i = 0; i < x.size(); ++i) d = std::max(d, std::fabs(x[i] - y[i]));
            return d;
        };
        float c_max = 0.0f;

        // (a) Default-constructed cache is exact and bit-identical to an explicit exact call
        SmartTieredCache c_exact(w1s.num_panels, 1);
        UHA_VERIFY(c_exact.is_exact());
        c_exact.execute_smart_w1_gemm(As, w1s, view(c_ref), /*force_exact=*/true);
        SmartTieredCache c_default(w1s.num_panels, 1);
        c_default.execute_smart_w1_gemm(As, w1s, view(c_def));
        UHA_VERIFY(c_def == c_ref);
        UHA_VERIFY(c_default.telemetry().tiles_pruned_by_energy_sieve == 0);
        for (float v : c_ref) c_max = std::max(c_max, std::fabs(v));

        // (b) Error budget: every output element stays within eps (plus float reassociation slack)
        const float eps = 0.05f;
        const float slack = 1e-5f * c_max;
        size_t budget_skips_w1 = 0, budget_skips_w2 = 0;
        float err_w1 = 0.0f, err_w2 = 0.0f;
        {
            SmartTieredCache c_b(w1s.num_panels, 1);
            c_b.set_error_budget(eps);
            UHA_VERIFY(!c_b.is_exact());
            c_b.execute_smart_w1_gemm(As, w1s, view(c_bud));
            budget_skips_w1 = c_b.telemetry().tiles_pruned_by_energy_sieve;
            err_w1 = max_abs_diff(c_bud, c_ref);
            UHA_VERIFY(err_w1 <= eps + slack);
        }
        {
            std::vector<float> c_ref2(Ms * Ns), c_bud2(Ms * Ns);
            SmartTieredCache c_e(w2s.num_panels, 1), c_b(w2s.num_panels, 1);
            c_e.execute_smart_w2_gemm(As, w2s, view(c_ref2));
            c_b.set_error_budget(eps);
            c_b.execute_smart_w2_gemm(As, w2s, view(c_bud2));
            budget_skips_w2 = c_b.telemetry().tiles_pruned_by_energy_sieve;
            err_w2 = max_abs_diff(c_bud2, c_ref2);
            UHA_VERIFY(err_w2 <= eps + slack);
        }
        UHA_VERIFY(budget_skips_w1 > 0 && budget_skips_w2 > 0); // the budget actually removes work

        // (c) Negative control: the legacy summed-energy heuristic drops group 1 and breaks the same budget
        SmartTieredCache c_t(w1s.num_panels, 1, /*tau=*/0.85f);
        c_t.execute_smart_w1_gemm(As, w1s, view(c_tau));
        const float err_tau = max_abs_diff(c_tau, c_ref);
        UHA_VERIFY(err_tau > eps);

        // (d) Shape mismatch is rejected instead of reading out of bounds
        bool threw = false;
        try {
            std::vector<float> bad(Ms * (Ns - 8));
            TensorView<float> v_bad(bad.data(), Shape{static_cast<int64_t>(Ms), static_cast<int64_t>(Ns - 8)});
            SmartTieredCache c_x(w1s.num_panels, 1);
            c_x.execute_smart_w1_gemm(As, w1s, v_bad);
        } catch (const std::invalid_argument&) {
            threw = true;
        }
        UHA_VERIFY(threw);

        std::printf("[8] Sieve policies (exact default, guaranteed error budget, legacy-heuristic control):\n");
        std::printf("    default == exact (bitwise), 0 tiles skipped | budget eps = %.3f: W1 skipped %zu tiles, max err %.3e; W2 skipped %zu, max err %.3e\n",
                    eps, budget_skips_w1, err_w1, budget_skips_w2, err_w2);
        std::printf("    legacy tau=0.85 heuristic on the same data: max err %.3e (> eps, loud single row dropped) | shape check -> PASS\n",
                    err_tau);
    }

    return 0;
}
```

## File: `sim_harness/ptx_bf162_emu.c`
```c
// File: sim_harness/ptx_bf162_emu.c
// Bit-Exact PTX & IEEE-754 bf162 Instruction Emulator
// Build: gcc -O3 -std=c11 -o ptx_bf162_emu ptx_bf162_emu.c -lm

#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <math.h>
#include <assert.h>

// ==============================================================================
// 1. IEEE-754 BFloat16 & FP32 Bit-Exact Conversion Helpers
// ==============================================================================

static inline uint32_t fp32_to_bits(float f) {
    uint32_t u;
    memcpy(&u, &f, sizeof(uint32_t));
    return u;
}

static inline float bits_to_fp32(uint32_t u) {
    float f;
    memcpy(&f, &u, sizeof(float));
    return f;
}

// IEEE-754 round-to-nearest-even FP32 -> BF16 conversion (matches PTX .rn.bf16)
static inline uint16_t fp32_to_bf16_rn(float f) {
    uint32_t u = fp32_to_bits(f);
    if ((u & 0x7F800000U) == 0x7F800000U && (u & 0x007FFFFFU) != 0) {
        return (uint16_t)((u >> 16) | 0x0040U); // Quiet NaN
    }
    uint32_t lsb = (u >> 16) & 1U;
    uint32_t rounding_bias = 0x00007FFFU + lsb;
    u += rounding_bias;
    return (uint16_t)(u >> 16);
}

static inline float bf16_to_fp32(uint16_t b) {
    uint32_t u = ((uint32_t)b) << 16;
    return bits_to_fp32(u);
}

static inline uint32_t pack_bf162(uint16_t low, uint16_t high) {
    return ((uint32_t)low) | (((uint32_t)high) << 16);
}

static inline uint16_t unpack_bf162_low(uint32_t pair) {
    return (uint16_t)(pair & 0xFFFFU);
}

static inline uint16_t unpack_bf162_high(uint32_t pair) {
    return (uint16_t)(pair >> 16);
}

// ==============================================================================
// 2. Bit-Exact PTX Instruction Emulators
// ==============================================================================

// PTX: shl.b32 d, a, b (clamped at 32 per PTX ISA specification)
static inline uint32_t ptx_shl_b32(uint32_t a, uint32_t b) {
    return (b >= 32U) ? 0U : (a << b);
}

// PTX: shr.u32 d, a, b (clamped at 32 per PTX ISA specification)
static inline uint32_t ptx_shr_u32(uint32_t a, uint32_t b) {
    return (b >= 32U) ? 0U : (a >> b);
}

// PTX: lop3.b32 d, a, b, c, immLut
// Evaluates arbitrary 3-input Boolean truth table specified by immLut (0x00..0xFF)
// where basis constants are TA = 0xF0, TB = 0xCC, TC = 0xAA.
static inline uint32_t ptx_lop3_b32(uint32_t a, uint32_t b, uint32_t c, uint8_t immLut) {
    uint32_t result = 0U;
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
        if ((immLut >> i) & 1U) {
            uint32_t term_a = (i & 4) ? a : ~a;
            uint32_t term_b = (i & 2) ? b : ~b;
            uint32_t term_c = (i & 1) ? c : ~c;
            result |= (term_a & term_b & term_c);
        }
    }
    return result;
}

// PTX: mul.rn.bf16x2 d, a, b (or fma.rn.bf16x2 d, a, b, -0.0)
static inline uint32_t ptx_mul_rn_bf162(uint32_t a, uint32_t b) {
    float a_lo = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_low(a));
    float a_hi = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_high(a));
    float b_lo = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_low(b));
    float b_hi = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_high(b));
    uint16_t d_lo = fp32_to_bf16_rn(a_lo * b_lo);
    uint16_t d_hi = fp32_to_bf16_rn(a_hi * b_hi);
    return pack_bf162(d_lo, d_hi);
}

// PTX: fma.rn.bf16x2 d, a, b, c
static inline uint32_t ptx_fma_rn_bf162(uint32_t a, uint32_t b, uint32_t c) {
    float a_lo = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_low(a));
    float a_hi = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_high(a));
    float b_lo = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_low(b));
    float b_hi = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_high(b));
    float c_lo = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_low(c));
    float c_hi = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_high(c));
    uint16_t d_lo = fp32_to_bf16_rn(fmaf(a_lo, b_lo, c_lo));
    uint16_t d_hi = fp32_to_bf16_rn(fmaf(a_hi, b_hi, c_hi));
    return pack_bf162(d_lo, d_hi);
}

// PTX: abs.bf16x2 d, a (clears IEEE-754 sign bits 15 and 31)
static inline uint32_t ptx_abs_bf162(uint32_t a) {
    return a & 0x7FFF7FFFU;
}

// PTX: add.rn.bf16x2 d, a, b (or fma.rn.bf16x2 d, a, 1.0, b)
static inline uint32_t ptx_add_rn_bf162(uint32_t a, uint32_t b) {
    float a_lo = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_low(a));
    float a_hi = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_high(a));
    float b_lo = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_low(b));
    float b_hi = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_high(b));
    return pack_bf162(fp32_to_bf16_rn(a_lo + b_lo), fp32_to_bf16_rn(a_hi + b_hi));
}

// PTX: max.bf16x2 d, a, b (used in Quest/LServe sign-aware page bounding boxes)
static inline uint32_t ptx_max_bf162(uint32_t a, uint32_t b) {
    float a_lo = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_low(a));
    float a_hi = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_high(a));
    float b_lo = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_low(b));
    float b_hi = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_high(b));
    uint16_t d_lo = (a_lo >= b_lo) ? unpack_bf162_low(a) : unpack_bf162_low(b);
    uint16_t d_hi = (a_hi >= b_hi) ? unpack_bf162_high(a) : unpack_bf162_high(b);
    return pack_bf162(d_lo, d_hi);
}

// PTX: prmt.b32 d, a, b, sel (FLUTE 4-byte register LUT selector across {a, b})
static inline uint32_t ptx_prmt_b32(uint32_t a, uint32_t b, uint32_t sel) {
    uint64_t combined = ((uint64_t)b << 32) | (uint64_t)a;
    uint32_t out = 0U;
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
        uint32_t ctrl = (sel >> (4 * i)) & 0xFU;
        uint32_t byte_idx = ctrl & 0x7U;
        uint8_t byte_val = (uint8_t)((combined >> (8 * byte_idx)) & 0xFFU);
        if (ctrl & 0x8U) {
            byte_val = (byte_val & 0x80U) ? 0xFFU : 0x00U; // Sign-extend mode
        }
        out |= ((uint32_t)byte_val) << (8 * i);
    }
    return out;
}

// Quest / LServe branchless 2-instruction PTX sign-aware page bounding-box inner product:
// Evaluates max(q * k_min, q * k_max) for two channels simultaneously in bf162.
static inline uint32_t ptx_quest_page_ub_bf162(
    uint32_t q_bf162,
    uint32_t k_min_bf162,
    uint32_t k_max_bf162
) {
    uint32_t p_min = ptx_mul_rn_bf162(q_bf162, k_min_bf162);
    uint32_t p_max = ptx_mul_rn_bf162(q_bf162, k_max_bf162);
    return ptx_max_bf162(p_min, p_max);
}

// ==============================================================================
// 3. Interleaved 16-Bit Split Packing & Branchless PTX Unpackers
// ==============================================================================

// Pack 32 1-bit weights b[0..31] (each in {0, 1} representing {-1, +1})
// into Interleaved 16-Bit Split Layout:
//   even weights b_{2k}   -> bit k      (k in 0..15)
//   odd weights  b_{2k+1} -> bit k + 16 (k in 0..15)
static inline uint32_t pack_w1_interleaved_u32(const uint8_t b[32]) {
    uint32_t w = 0U;
    for (int k = 0; k < 16; ++k) {
        uint32_t even_bit = (uint32_t)(b[2 * k] & 1U);
        uint32_t odd_bit  = (uint32_t)(b[2 * k + 1] & 1U);
        w |= (even_bit << k) | (odd_bit << (k + 16));
    }
    return w;
}

// Branchless 3-instruction PTX 1-bit unpacker for pair k in [0..15]
static inline uint32_t ptx_unpack_w1_pair(uint32_t w_interleaved, uint32_t k, uint32_t scale_bf162) {
    uint32_t tmp = ptx_shl_b32(w_interleaved, 15U - k);
    uint32_t unscaled = ptx_lop3_b32(tmp, 0x80008000U, 0x3F803F80U, 0xAEU);
    return ptx_mul_rn_bf162(unscaled, scale_bf162);
}

// Pack 16 2-bit ternary weights q[0..15] (each in {0, 1, 2} representing {-1, 0, +1})
// into Interleaved 16-Bit Split Layout:
//   even weights q_{2k}   -> bits [2k+1 : 2k]     in lower 16 bits (k in 0..7)
//   odd weights  q_{2k+1} -> bits [2k+17 : 2k+16] in upper 16 bits (k in 0..7)
static inline uint32_t pack_w2_ternary_interleaved_u32(const uint8_t q[16]) {
    uint32_t w = 0U;
    for (int k = 0; k < 8; ++k) {
        uint32_t even_2b = (uint32_t)(q[2 * k] & 3U);
        uint32_t odd_2b  = (uint32_t)(q[2 * k + 1] & 3U);
        w |= (even_2b << (2 * k)) | (odd_2b << (2 * k + 16));
    }
    return w;
}

// Branchless PTX 2-bit ternary unpacker for pair k in [0..7]
// Uses exact unscaled BF162 constants c_mul = (64.0, 64.0) [0x42804280] and
// c_sub = (-129.0, -129.0) [0xC301C301] in fma.rn.bf16x2 to produce exact unit
// trits {-1.0, 0.0, +1.0} [0xBF80, 0x0000, 0x3F80], followed by fma.rn.bf16x2 with
// scale_bf162. This prevents 8-bit BF16 significand overflow on (-129.0 * alpha_g)
// and guarantees that ternary zero (q = 1) unpacks to bit-exact +0.0 (0x0000)
// across all 65,536 possible BF16 scales.
static inline uint32_t ptx_unpack_w2_ternary_pair(
    uint32_t w_tern_interleaved,
    uint32_t k,
    uint32_t scale_bf162
) {
    const uint32_t c_mul_64_bf162  = 0x42804280U; // (64.0f, 64.0f) in BF162
    const uint32_t c_sub_129_bf162 = 0xC301C301U; // (-129.0f, -129.0f) in BF162
    uint32_t tmp = ptx_shr_u32(w_tern_interleaved, 2U * k);
    uint32_t injected = ptx_lop3_b32(tmp, 0x00030003U, 0x40004000U, 0xEAU);
    uint32_t unit_trit = ptx_fma_rn_bf162(injected, c_mul_64_bf162, c_sub_129_bf162);
    return ptx_mul_rn_bf162(unit_trit, scale_bf162);
}

// ==============================================================================
// 4. Warp-Level mma.sync.aligned.m16n8k16.row.col.f32.bf16.bf16.f32 Emulator
// ==============================================================================

typedef struct {
    uint32_t a[4]; // 4 bf162 registers per thread (8 BF16 activations)
    uint32_t b[2]; // 2 bf162 registers per thread (4 BF16 weights)
    float    c[4]; // 4 FP32 accumulator registers per thread
    float    d[4]; // 4 FP32 output registers per thread
} WarpThreadRegs;

// Emulates a full 32-lane warp executing mma.sync.aligned.m16n8k16.row.col.f32.bf16.bf16.f32
// using exact warp lane-to-matrix coordinate mapping from the PTX ISA documentation:
//   For A (16x16, row-major):
//     groupID = lane_id >> 2 (0..7), tid_in_group = lane_id & 3 (0..3)
//     a[0] holds (row = groupID,     col = 2*tid_in_group .. 2*tid_in_group+1)
//     a[1] holds (row = groupID + 8, col = 2*tid_in_group .. 2*tid_in_group+1)
//     a[2] holds (row = groupID,     col = 2*tid_in_group + 8 .. 2*tid_in_group+9)
//     a[3] holds (row = groupID + 8, col = 2*tid_in_group + 8 .. 2*tid_in_group+9)
//   For B (16x8, col-major, K=16, N=8):
//     col n = groupID (0..7)
//     b[0] holds (row k = 2*tid_in_group .. 2*tid_in_group+1,     col = groupID)
//     b[1] holds (row k = 2*tid_in_group + 8 .. 2*tid_in_group+9, col = groupID)
//   For C/D (16x8, row-major):
//     d[0], d[1] hold (row = groupID,     col = 2*tid_in_group, 2*tid_in_group+1)
//     d[2], d[3] hold (row = groupID + 8, col = 2*tid_in_group, 2*tid_in_group+1)
static void ptx_mma_m16n8k16_bf16_f32_warp(WarpThreadRegs warp[32]) {
    float A_mat[16][16];
    float B_mat[16][8];
    float C_mat[16][8];

    for (int lane = 0; lane < 32; ++lane) {
        int group_id = lane >> 2;
        int tid = lane & 3;

        A_mat[group_id][2 * tid]         = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_low(warp[lane].a[0]));
        A_mat[group_id][2 * tid + 1]     = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_high(warp[lane].a[0]));
        A_mat[group_id + 8][2 * tid]     = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_low(warp[lane].a[1]));
        A_mat[group_id + 8][2 * tid + 1] = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_high(warp[lane].a[1]));
        A_mat[group_id][2 * tid + 8]     = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_low(warp[lane].a[2]));
        A_mat[group_id][2 * tid + 9]     = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_high(warp[lane].a[2]));
        A_mat[group_id + 8][2 * tid + 8] = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_low(warp[lane].a[3]));
        A_mat[group_id + 8][2 * tid + 9] = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_high(warp[lane].a[3]));

        B_mat[2 * tid][group_id]         = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_low(warp[lane].b[0]));
        B_mat[2 * tid + 1][group_id]     = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_high(warp[lane].b[0]));
        B_mat[2 * tid + 8][group_id]     = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_low(warp[lane].b[1]));
        B_mat[2 * tid + 9][group_id]     = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_high(warp[lane].b[1]));

        C_mat[group_id][2 * tid]         = warp[lane].c[0];
        C_mat[group_id][2 * tid + 1]     = warp[lane].c[1];
        C_mat[group_id + 8][2 * tid]     = warp[lane].c[2];
        C_mat[group_id + 8][2 * tid + 1] = warp[lane].c[3];
    }

    float D_mat[16][8];
    for (int m = 0; m < 16; ++m) {
        for (int n = 0; n < 8; ++n) {
            float acc = C_mat[m][n];
            for (int k = 0; k < 16; ++k) {
                acc += A_mat[m][k] * B_mat[k][n];
            }
            D_mat[m][n] = acc;
        }
    }

    for (int lane = 0; lane < 32; ++lane) {
        int group_id = lane >> 2;
        int tid = lane & 3;
        warp[lane].d[0] = D_mat[group_id][2 * tid];
        warp[lane].d[1] = D_mat[group_id][2 * tid + 1];
        warp[lane].d[2] = D_mat[group_id + 8][2 * tid];
        warp[lane].d[3] = D_mat[group_id + 8][2 * tid + 1];
    }
}

// ==============================================================================
// 5. Exhaustive Verification Suite
// ==============================================================================

int main(void) {
    printf("================================================================================\n");
    printf("  WORKSTREAM 1: BIT-EXACT PTX & IEEE-754 BF162 INSTRUCTION EMULATOR\n");
    printf("================================================================================\n\n");

    // 1. Verify truth-table basis derivation for immLut = 0xAE and immLut = 0xEA
    const uint8_t TA = 0xF0U;
    const uint8_t TB = 0xCCU;
    const uint8_t TC = 0xAAU;
    uint8_t lut_1bit = (uint8_t)(((~TA) & TB) | TC);
    uint8_t lut_2bit = (uint8_t)((TA & TB) | TC);

    printf("[1] Truth-Table Basis Derivation Check:\n");
    printf("    TA = 0x%02X, TB = 0x%02X, TC = 0x%02X\n", TA, TB, TC);
    printf("    F_1bit(a,b,c) = (~a & b) | c  -> immLut = 0x%02X (Expected: 0xAE)\n", lut_1bit);
    printf("    F_2bit(a,b,c) = (a & b)  | c  -> immLut = 0x%02X (Expected: 0xEA)\n\n", lut_2bit);
    assert(lut_1bit == 0xAEU);
    assert(lut_2bit == 0xEAU);

    // 2. Exhaustive verification of lop3.b32 (0xAE) across all 2^16 adjacent noise states
    //    and all 16 pair positions k in [0..15] (16 * 4 * 65,536 = 4,194,304 checks)
    printf("[2] Exhaustive 1-Bit Binary g128 Unpacker Check (immLut = 0xAE):\n");
    uint64_t total_w1_tests = 0;
    uint64_t w1_bit_errors = 0;

    float test_scales[4] = {1.0f, 0.03125f, 0.142578125f, 1.75f};
    for (int s_idx = 0; s_idx < 4; ++s_idx) {
        uint16_t scale_bf16 = fp32_to_bf16_rn(test_scales[s_idx]);
        uint32_t scale_bf162 = pack_bf162(scale_bf16, scale_bf16);
        uint16_t pos_ref = fp32_to_bf16_rn(+1.0f * bf16_to_fp32(scale_bf16));
        uint16_t neg_ref = fp32_to_bf16_rn(-1.0f * bf16_to_fp32(scale_bf16));

        for (uint32_t k = 0; k < 16; ++k) {
            for (uint32_t b_even = 0; b_even <= 1; ++b_even) {
                for (uint32_t b_odd = 0; b_odd <= 1; ++b_odd) {
                    uint32_t target_mask = (1U << k) | (1U << (k + 16));
                    uint32_t target_bits = (b_even << k) | (b_odd << (k + 16));
                    uint32_t expected_pair = pack_bf162(
                        b_even ? pos_ref : neg_ref,
                        b_odd  ? pos_ref : neg_ref
                    );

                    // Sweep all 2^16 = 65,536 states across surrounding bit positions
                    for (uint32_t noise_state = 0; noise_state < 65536U; ++noise_state) {
                        uint32_t noise_word = (noise_state * 0x9E3779B9U) & (~target_mask);
                        uint32_t w_interleaved = target_bits | noise_word;
                        uint32_t actual_pair = ptx_unpack_w1_pair(w_interleaved, k, scale_bf162);
                        if (actual_pair != expected_pair) {
                            w1_bit_errors++;
                        }
                        total_w1_tests++;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    printf("    Tested States : %llu (all 2^16 noise states x 4 bit pairs x 16 k-shifts x 4 scales)\n",
           (unsigned long long)total_w1_tests);
    printf("    Bit Errors    : %llu (rtol = 0, atol = 0)\n\n", (unsigned long long)w1_bit_errors);
    assert(w1_bit_errors == 0);

    // 3. Exhaustive verification of lop3.b32 (0xEA) + 2x fma.rn.bf16x2 across all
    //    ternary pairs q in {0, 1, 2}^2, all k in [0..7], and all 2^16 surrounding bit states
    printf("[3] Exhaustive 2-Bit Ternary (BitNet b1.58) Unpacker Check (immLut = 0xEA + fma.rn.bf16x2):\n");
    uint64_t total_w2_tests = 0;
    uint64_t w2_bit_errors = 0;

    for (int s_idx = 0; s_idx < 4; ++s_idx) {
        uint16_t scale_bf16 = fp32_to_bf16_rn(test_scales[s_idx]);
        float alpha_g = bf16_to_fp32(scale_bf16);
        uint32_t scale_bf162 = pack_bf162(scale_bf16, scale_bf16);

        // True mathematical ternary target: {-alpha_g, +0.0f, +alpha_g}
        uint16_t ref_map[3] = {
            fp32_to_bf16_rn(-1.0f * alpha_g),
            0x0000U, // Exact +0.0f in BF16 for q = 1 (zero weight)
            fp32_to_bf16_rn(+1.0f * alpha_g)
        };
        assert(bf16_to_fp32(ref_map[1]) == 0.0f);

        for (uint32_t k = 0; k < 8; ++k) {
            for (uint32_t q_even = 0; q_even <= 2; ++q_even) {
                for (uint32_t q_odd = 0; q_odd <= 2; ++q_odd) {
                    uint32_t target_mask = (3U << (2 * k)) | (3U << (2 * k + 16));
                    uint32_t target_bits = (q_even << (2 * k)) | (q_odd << (2 * k + 16));
                    uint32_t expected_pair = pack_bf162(ref_map[q_even], ref_map[q_odd]);

                    for (uint32_t noise_state = 0; noise_state < 65536U; ++noise_state) {
                        uint32_t noise_word = (noise_state * 0x85EBCA6BU) & (~target_mask);
                        uint32_t w_tern = target_bits | noise_word;
                        uint32_t actual_pair = ptx_unpack_w2_ternary_pair(
                            w_tern, k, scale_bf162
                        );
                        if (actual_pair != expected_pair) {
                            w2_bit_errors++;
                        }
                        total_w2_tests++;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    printf("    Tested States : %llu (all 2^16 noise states x 9 ternary pairs x 8 k-shifts x 4 scales)\n",
           (unsigned long long)total_w2_tests);
    printf("    Bit Errors    : %llu (rtol = 0, atol = 0)\n\n", (unsigned long long)w2_bit_errors);
    assert(w2_bit_errors == 0);

    // 4. Warp-level mma.sync.aligned.m16n8k16.row.col.f32.bf16.bf16.f32 + W1A16 K=32 step verification
    printf("[4] Warp-Level Fused W1A16_F32 mma.sync.aligned.m16n8k16 Verification (K=32):\n");
    float A_ref[16][32];
    uint8_t W_bits[8][32]; // 8 columns (N=8), 32 rows (K=32)
    float scale_n[8];
    uint32_t w_packed_col[8];
    uint32_t scale_col_bf162[8];

    uint32_t rng = 0x12345678U;
    for (int m = 0; m < 16; ++m) {
        for (int k = 0; k < 32; ++k) {
            rng = rng * 1664525U + 1013904223U;
            float val = ((float)((int32_t)(rng >> 16) - 32768)) / 16384.0f;
            A_ref[m][k] = bf16_to_fp32(fp32_to_bf16_rn(val));
        }
    }

    for (int n = 0; n < 8; ++n) {
        rng = rng * 1664525U + 1013904223U;
        uint16_t sc_bf16 = fp32_to_bf16_rn(0.125f + (float)(n + 1) * 0.0625f);
        scale_n[n] = bf16_to_fp32(sc_bf16);
        scale_col_bf162[n] = pack_bf162(sc_bf16, sc_bf16);
        for (int k = 0; k < 32; ++k) {
            rng = rng * 1664525U + 1013904223U;
            W_bits[n][k] = (uint8_t)((rng >> 24) & 1U);
        }
        w_packed_col[n] = pack_w1_interleaved_u32(W_bits[n]);
    }

    WarpThreadRegs warp[32];
    memset(warp, 0, sizeof(warp));

    // Sub-step 0 (K = 0..15) and Sub-step 1 (K = 16..31)
    for (int sub = 0; sub < 2; ++sub) {
        int k_base = sub * 16;
        for (int lane = 0; lane < 32; ++lane) {
            int group_id = lane >> 2;
            int t = lane & 3;

            // Load A fragment for this lane
            warp[lane].a[0] = pack_bf162(
                fp32_to_bf16_rn(A_ref[group_id][k_base + 2 * t]),
                fp32_to_bf16_rn(A_ref[group_id][k_base + 2 * t + 1])
            );
            warp[lane].a[1] = pack_bf162(
                fp32_to_bf16_rn(A_ref[group_id + 8][k_base + 2 * t]),
                fp32_to_bf16_rn(A_ref[group_id + 8][k_base + 2 * t + 1])
            );
            warp[lane].a[2] = pack_bf162(
                fp32_to_bf16_rn(A_ref[group_id][k_base + 2 * t + 8]),
                fp32_to_bf16_rn(A_ref[group_id][k_base + 2 * t + 9])
            );
            warp[lane].a[3] = pack_bf162(
                fp32_to_bf16_rn(A_ref[group_id + 8][k_base + 2 * t + 8]),
                fp32_to_bf16_rn(A_ref[group_id + 8][k_base + 2 * t + 9])
            );

            // Unpack B fragment using exact pair indices p0 = t + 8*sub, p1 = t + 4 + 8*sub
            uint32_t p0 = (uint32_t)(t + 8 * sub);
            uint32_t p1 = (uint32_t)(t + 4 + 8 * sub);
            warp[lane].b[0] = ptx_unpack_w1_pair(w_packed_col[group_id], p0, scale_col_bf162[group_id]);
            warp[lane].b[1] = ptx_unpack_w1_pair(w_packed_col[group_id], p1, scale_col_bf162[group_id]);
        }

        ptx_mma_m16n8k16_bf16_f32_warp(warp);
        for (int lane = 0; lane < 32; ++lane) {
            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
                warp[lane].c[r] = warp[lane].d[r];
            }
        }
    }

    // Verify accumulated warp registers against direct reference matrix multiplication
    int mma_mismatches = 0;
    for (int lane = 0; lane < 32; ++lane) {
        int group_id = lane >> 2;
        int t = lane & 3;
        int rows[4] = {group_id, group_id, group_id + 8, group_id + 8};
        int cols[4] = {2 * t, 2 * t + 1, 2 * t, 2 * t + 1};

        for (int idx = 0; idx < 4; ++idx) {
            int m = rows[idx];
            int n = cols[idx];
            float ref_acc = 0.0f;
            for (int k = 0; k < 32; ++k) {
                float w_val = (W_bits[n][k] ? +1.0f : -1.0f) * scale_n[n];
                ref_acc += A_ref[m][k] * w_val;
            }
            if (warp[lane].d[idx] != ref_acc) {
                mma_mismatches++;
            }
        }
    }

    printf("    Warp Accumulator Elements Checked : 128 (16x8 FP32 tile over K=32)\n");
    printf("    Warp Accumulator Bit Mismatches   : %d (rtol = 0, atol = 0)\n\n", mma_mismatches);
    assert(mma_mismatches == 0);

    // 5. Warp-level Fused 2-Stage Kronecker H_32 = H_2 x H_16 + W2A16_F32 Ternary MMA Verification (K=32, fixes O12)
    printf("[5] Warp-Level Fused 2-Stage HadaCore Kronecker (H_2 x H_16) + W2A16 Ternary MMA (K=32):\n");
    uint8_t W2_trits[8][32]; // values in {0, 1, 2} representing {-1, 0, +1}
    uint32_t w2_packed_col[8][2]; // 2 uint32 words (16 trits each) per N-column for K=32
    for (int n = 0; n < 8; ++n) {
        for (int k = 0; k < 32; ++k) {
            rng = rng * 1664525U + 1013904223U;
            W2_trits[n][k] = (uint8_t)((rng >> 24) % 3U);
        }
        w2_packed_col[n][0] = pack_w2_ternary_interleaved_u32(&W2_trits[n][0]);
        w2_packed_col[n][1] = pack_w2_ternary_interleaved_u32(&W2_trits[n][16]);
    }

    // Build normalized 16x16 Sylvester-Hadamard matrix H16_norm[k][c] = (-1)^popcount(k & c) * 0.25f
    float H16_norm[16][16];
    for (int k = 0; k < 16; ++k) {
        for (int c = 0; c < 16; ++c) {
            int v = k & c;
            int pop = ((v >> 3) & 1) + ((v >> 2) & 1) + ((v >> 1) & 1) + (v & 1);
            H16_norm[k][c] = (pop & 1) ? -0.25f : +0.25f;
        }
    }

    // Reference 2-stage Kronecker transform (orthonormal H_32 = H_2 (x) H_16):
    //   Stage 1: U_sub[m][c] = bf16_rn(sum_{k=0..15} A_ref[m][sub*16 + k] * H16_norm[k][c])
    //   Stage 2: s0 = bf16_rn(U_0[m][c] * (1/sqrt(2)))
    //            A_kron_ref[m][c]      = bf16_rn(fma(U_1[m][c], +1/sqrt(2), s0))
    //            A_kron_ref[m][16 + c] = bf16_rn(fma(U_1[m][c], -1/sqrt(2), s0))
    const uint32_t h2_pos_bf162 = 0x3F353F35U; // (+0.70703125f, +0.70703125f) ~= +1/sqrt(2) in BF162
    const uint32_t h2_neg_bf162 = 0xBF35BF35U; // (-0.70703125f, -0.70703125f) ~= -1/sqrt(2) in BF162
    float U_ref[2][16][16];
    float A_kron_ref[16][32];
    for (int sub = 0; sub < 2; ++sub) {
        for (int m = 0; m < 16; ++m) {
            for (int c = 0; c < 16; ++c) {
                float acc = 0.0f;
                for (int k = 0; k < 16; ++k) {
                    acc += A_ref[m][sub * 16 + k] * H16_norm[k][c];
                }
                U_ref[sub][m][c] = bf16_to_fp32(fp32_to_bf16_rn(acc));
            }
        }
    }
    for (int m = 0; m < 16; ++m) {
        for (int c = 0; c < 16; ++c) {
            uint32_t u0_b = (uint32_t)fp32_to_bf16_rn(U_ref[0][m][c]);
            uint32_t u1_b = (uint32_t)fp32_to_bf16_rn(U_ref[1][m][c]);
            uint32_t s0_b = ptx_mul_rn_bf162(u0_b, h2_pos_bf162);
            uint32_t r0_b = ptx_fma_rn_bf162(u1_b, h2_pos_bf162, s0_b);
            uint32_t r1_b = ptx_fma_rn_bf162(u1_b, h2_neg_bf162, s0_b);
            A_kron_ref[m][c]      = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_low(r0_b));
            A_kron_ref[m][16 + c] = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_low(r1_b));
        }
    }

    // Execute Stage 1 on the emulated Tensor Core warp: two m16n8k16 MMAs per K=16 sub-tile
    uint32_t u_stage1_regs[2][32][4]; // [sub 0..1][lane 0..31][reg 0..3]
    for (int sub = 0; sub < 2; ++sub) {
        int k_base = sub * 16;
        for (int half = 0; half < 2; ++half) { // half=0 -> cols 0..7 of H16, half=1 -> cols 8..15 of H16
            WarpThreadRegs warp_h16[32];
            memset(warp_h16, 0, sizeof(warp_h16));
            for (int lane = 0; lane < 32; ++lane) {
                int group_id = lane >> 2;
                int t = lane & 3;
                warp_h16[lane].a[0] = pack_bf162(fp32_to_bf16_rn(A_ref[group_id][k_base + 2 * t]),     fp32_to_bf16_rn(A_ref[group_id][k_base + 2 * t + 1]));
                warp_h16[lane].a[1] = pack_bf162(fp32_to_bf16_rn(A_ref[group_id + 8][k_base + 2 * t]), fp32_to_bf16_rn(A_ref[group_id + 8][k_base + 2 * t + 1]));
                warp_h16[lane].a[2] = pack_bf162(fp32_to_bf16_rn(A_ref[group_id][k_base + 2 * t + 8]), fp32_to_bf16_rn(A_ref[group_id][k_base + 2 * t + 9]));
                warp_h16[lane].a[3] = pack_bf162(fp32_to_bf16_rn(A_ref[group_id + 8][k_base + 2 * t + 8]), fp32_to_bf16_rn(A_ref[group_id + 8][k_base + 2 * t + 9]));

                int h_col = half * 8 + group_id;
                warp_h16[lane].b[0] = pack_bf162(fp32_to_bf16_rn(H16_norm[2 * t][h_col]),     fp32_to_bf16_rn(H16_norm[2 * t + 1][h_col]));
                warp_h16[lane].b[1] = pack_bf162(fp32_to_bf16_rn(H16_norm[2 * t + 8][h_col]), fp32_to_bf16_rn(H16_norm[2 * t + 9][h_col]));
            }
            ptx_mma_m16n8k16_bf16_f32_warp(warp_h16);
            for (int lane = 0; lane < 32; ++lane) {
                u_stage1_regs[sub][lane][2 * half + 0] = pack_bf162(
                    fp32_to_bf16_rn(warp_h16[lane].d[0]), fp32_to_bf16_rn(warp_h16[lane].d[1])
                );
                u_stage1_regs[sub][lane][2 * half + 1] = pack_bf162(
                    fp32_to_bf16_rn(warp_h16[lane].d[2]), fp32_to_bf16_rn(warp_h16[lane].d[3])
                );
            }
        }
    }

    // Execute Stage 2 (in-register H_2 butterfly via ptx_fma_rn_bf162) + Stage 3 (W2A16 Ternary MMA)
    WarpThreadRegs warp_w2[32];
    memset(warp_w2, 0, sizeof(warp_w2));

    for (int sub = 0; sub < 2; ++sub) {
        for (int lane = 0; lane < 32; ++lane) {
            int group_id = lane >> 2;
            int t = lane & 3;
            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
                uint32_t scaled_0 = ptx_mul_rn_bf162(u_stage1_regs[0][lane][r], h2_pos_bf162);
                warp_w2[lane].a[r] = ptx_fma_rn_bf162(
                    u_stage1_regs[1][lane][r], (sub == 0) ? h2_pos_bf162 : h2_neg_bf162, scaled_0
                );
            }

            // Unpack 2-bit ternary B fragment for sub-step (8 pairs per uint32 word: p0 = t, p1 = t + 4)
            warp_w2[lane].b[0] = ptx_unpack_w2_ternary_pair(w2_packed_col[group_id][sub], (uint32_t)t, scale_col_bf162[group_id]);
            warp_w2[lane].b[1] = ptx_unpack_w2_ternary_pair(w2_packed_col[group_id][sub], (uint32_t)(t + 4), scale_col_bf162[group_id]);
        }

        ptx_mma_m16n8k16_bf16_f32_warp(warp_w2);
        for (int lane = 0; lane < 32; ++lane) {
            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
                warp_w2[lane].c[r] = warp_w2[lane].d[r];
            }
        }
    }

    int w2_mma_mismatches = 0;
    for (int lane = 0; lane < 32; ++lane) {
        int group_id = lane >> 2;
        int t = lane & 3;
        int rows[4] = {group_id, group_id, group_id + 8, group_id + 8};
        int cols[4] = {2 * t, 2 * t + 1, 2 * t, 2 * t + 1};
        for (int idx = 0; idx < 4; ++idx) {
            int m = rows[idx];
            int n = cols[idx];
            float ref_acc = 0.0f;
            for (int k = 0; k < 32; ++k) {
                float tr = (float)((int)W2_trits[n][k] - 1);
                float w_val = bf16_to_fp32(fp32_to_bf16_rn(tr * scale_n[n]));
                ref_acc += A_kron_ref[m][k] * w_val;
            }
            if (warp_w2[lane].d[idx] != ref_acc) {
                w2_mma_mismatches++;
            }
        }
    }
    printf("    W2A16 Kronecker Accumulator Elements : 128 (16x8 FP32 tile over K=32)\n");
    printf("    W2A16 Kronecker Bit Mismatches       : %d (rtol = 0, atol = 0)\n\n", w2_mma_mismatches);
    assert(w2_mma_mismatches == 0);

    // 6. Quest Sign-Aware Page Bounding-Box & FLUTE prmt.b32 Register LUT Verification
    printf("[6] Quest Sign-Aware Page Bounding-Box & FLUTE prmt.b32 Register LUT Check:\n");
    int quest_errors = 0;
    float q_vals[4]   = {-2.5f, -0.5f, +0.75f, +3.0f};
    float min_vals[4] = {-4.0f, -1.5f, +0.25f, +1.0f};
    float max_vals[4] = {-1.0f, +2.0f, +1.50f, +5.0f};
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
        for (int j = 0; j < 4; ++j) {
            uint16_t q_b   = fp32_to_bf16_rn(q_vals[i]);
            uint16_t min_b = fp32_to_bf16_rn(min_vals[j]);
            uint16_t max_b = fp32_to_bf16_rn(max_vals[j]);
            uint32_t ub_pair = ptx_quest_page_ub_bf162(
                pack_bf162(q_b, q_b), pack_bf162(min_b, min_b), pack_bf162(max_b, max_b)
            );
            float actual_ub = bf16_to_fp32(unpack_bf162_low(ub_pair));
            float p0 = bf16_to_fp32(fp32_to_bf16_rn(bf16_to_fp32(q_b) * bf16_to_fp32(min_b)));
            float p1 = bf16_to_fp32(fp32_to_bf16_rn(bf16_to_fp32(q_b) * bf16_to_fp32(max_b)));
            float expected_ub = (p0 >= p1) ? p0 : p1;
            if (actual_ub != expected_ub) {
                quest_errors++;
            }
        }
    }
    uint32_t lut_lo = 0x3F800000U; // [0.0f, +1.0f] in BF16
    uint32_t lut_hi = 0xBF804000U; // [+2.0f, -1.0f] in BF16
    // Select bytes (7, 6) = 0xBF80 (-1.0f) and bytes (3, 2) = 0x3F80 (+1.0f) via prmt.b32
    uint32_t prmt_out = ptx_prmt_b32(lut_lo, lut_hi, 0x7632U);
    assert(prmt_out == 0xBF803F80U);
    printf("    Quest Bounding-Box & prmt.b32 Errors : %d (rtol = 0, atol = 0)\n\n", quest_errors);
    assert(quest_errors == 0);

    // Print Summary Verification Table
    printf("+----------------------------------------------+------------------+------------+--------+\n");
    printf("| Verification Target                          | States Tested    | Bit Errors | Status |\n");
    printf("+----------------------------------------------+------------------+------------+--------+\n");
    printf("| lop3.b32 (0xAE) Binary g128 -> bf162         | %16llu | %10llu | PASS   |\n",
           (unsigned long long)total_w1_tests, (unsigned long long)w1_bit_errors);
    printf("| lop3.b32 (0xEA) + fma.rn.bf16x2 Ternary      | %16llu | %10llu | PASS   |\n",
           (unsigned long long)total_w2_tests, (unsigned long long)w2_bit_errors);
    printf("| mma.sync.aligned.m16n8k16 W1A16_F32 (K=32)   | %16d | %10d | PASS   |\n",
           128, mma_mismatches);
    printf("| Kronecker (H2xH16) + W2A16 Ternary (K=32)    | %16d | %10d | PASS   |\n",
           128, w2_mma_mismatches);
    printf("| Quest Page Bounding + FLUTE prmt.b32 LUT     | %16d | %10d | PASS   |\n",
           17, quest_errors);
    printf("+----------------------------------------------+------------------+------------+--------+\n");

    return 0;
}
```

## File: `sim_harness/smsp_roofline_sim.py`
```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
File: sim_harness/smsp_roofline_sim.py
Cycle-Accurate SMSP Roofline & Scoreboard Simulator for GA102 (sm_86) and AD102 (sm_89).
Models:
  - 4 SMSP warp schedulers per SM (N_SMSP = 4)
  - 32-bank Shared Memory crossbar (32 banks x 4B = 128 Bytes/cycle/SM)
  - cp.async.cg.shared.global L2/DRAM multi-stage arrival pipeline
  - ldmatrix.sync.aligned.m8n8.x4.shared.b16 (512 Bytes = 4 SM cycles conflict-free)
  - HMMA.16816.F32 Tensor Core issue interval: tau = 16 cycles (Locked) vs tau = 8 cycles (Unlocked)
  - Fused W1A16_F32 (Binary g128, 1.125 bpw, 14.22x compression) vs Dense W16A16 (BF16)
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List, Dict


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class GPUArchSpec:
    name: str
    arch: str
    sm_count: int
    clock_mhz: float
    dram_bw_gbps: float
    sram_bytes_per_sm: int = 102400       # 100 KB total SRAM per SM
    max_dyn_sram_bytes: int = 101376      # 99 KB opt-in dynamic Shared Memory
    smsp_per_sm: int = 4
    sram_banks: int = 32
    bytes_per_bank_per_cycle: int = 4

    @property
    def sram_bw_bytes_per_cycle(self) -> int:
        return self.sram_banks * self.bytes_per_bank_per_cycle  # 128 B/cycle/SM

    def peak_tflops(self, tau_cycles: int) -> float:
        # Locked (tau=16): 512 FLOPs/cycle/SM; Unlocked (tau=8): 1,024 FLOPs/cycle/SM
        flops_per_sm_per_cycle = (2048.0 / tau_cycles) * self.smsp_per_sm
        return (self.sm_count * flops_per_sm_per_cycle * (self.clock_mhz * 1e6)) / 1e12


GA102_RTX_3090 = GPUArchSpec(
    name="RTX 3090 (GA102)",
    arch="sm_86",
    sm_count=82,
    clock_mhz=1691.2,
    dram_bw_gbps=936.2,
)

GA102_RTX_3090_TI = GPUArchSpec(
    name="RTX 3090 Ti (GA102)",
    arch="sm_86",
    sm_count=84,
    clock_mhz=1830.0,
    dram_bw_gbps=1008.0,
)

AD102_RTX_4090 = GPUArchSpec(
    name="RTX 4090 (AD102)",
    arch="sm_89",
    sm_count=128,
    clock_mhz=2530.0,
    dram_bw_gbps=1008.0,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TileConfig:
    label: str
    block_m: int
    block_n: int
    block_k: int
    warp_m: int
    warp_n: int
    num_warps: int
    num_stages: int
    beta_conflict: float = 1.0
    fused_w1a16: bool = False

    @property
    def dynamic_smem_bytes(self) -> int:
        if self.fused_w1a16:
            # Activations A in BF16 (2B), Weights B in 1.125 bpw (9/64 bytes/weight)
            a_bytes = self.block_m * self.block_k * 2
            b_bytes = int((self.block_n * self.block_k * 9) // 64)
            return (a_bytes + b_bytes) * self.num_stages
        return (self.block_m + self.block_n) * self.block_k * 2 * self.num_stages

    @property
    def warp_arithmetic_intensity(self) -> float:
        # FLOPs per K_step=16 is 2 * W_M * W_N * 16 = 32 * W_M * W_N
        flops = 32.0 * self.warp_m * self.warp_n
        if self.fused_w1a16:
            # Over K_step=16: A is W_M * 16 * 2B = 32 * W_M bytes;
            # B is compressed 14.222x: (32 * W_N) / 14.2222 = 2.25 * W_N bytes
            sram_bytes = 32.0 * self.warp_m + 2.25 * self.warp_n
        else:
            sram_bytes = 32.0 * (self.warp_m + self.warp_n)
        return flops / sram_bytes


def simulate_smsp_cycle_accurate(
    gpu: GPUArchSpec,
    cfg: TileConfig,
    tau_cycles: int,
    k_iters: int = 64,
) -> Dict[str, float]:
    """
    Cycle-accurate discrete-event simulation of 1 SM (4 SMSPs, 32-bank SRAM crossbar,
    multi-stage cp.async pipeline, ldmatrix unit, and Tensor Core MMA pipe).
    Simulates k_iters steps of K_step = 16.
    """
    hmma_per_warp_step = (cfg.warp_m // 16) * (cfg.warp_n // 8)
    # Analytical cycle equations per K_step = 16 across N_w warps:
    if cfg.fused_w1a16:
        bytes_ldmatrix = cfg.num_warps * (32.0 * cfg.warp_m + 2.25 * cfg.warp_n)
    else:
        bytes_ldmatrix = cfg.num_warps * 32.0 * (cfg.warp_m + cfg.warp_n)

    t_sram_step = (bytes_ldmatrix / gpu.sram_bw_bytes_per_cycle) * cfg.beta_conflict
    t_mma_step = (cfg.num_warps * hmma_per_warp_step / gpu.smsp_per_sm) * tau_cycles

    # Discrete-event simulation across k_iters steps with cp.async stage ring buffer
    sram_Crossbar_free_cycle = 0.0
    smsp_free_cycle = [0.0 for _ in range(gpu.smsp_per_sm)]
    total_smsp_busy_cycles = 0.0
    total_smsp_stall_cycles = 0.0

    # Pipeline stage latency penalty when num_stages < 4 on unlocked core (tau=8)
    stage_refill_latency = max(0.0, (4 - cfg.num_stages) * 14.0 * (16.0 / tau_cycles))

    for step in range(k_iters):
        # 1. cp.async stage arrival time
        stage_ready_cycle = 0.0
        if step >= cfg.num_stages:
            stage_ready_cycle = sram_Crossbar_free_cycle + stage_refill_latency

        # 2. Warps issue ldmatrix across shared 32-bank SRAM crossbar once both the crossbar
        #    and the earliest warp scheduler are ready for the next K_step=16 wave
        earliest_smsp_ready = min(smsp_free_cycle)
        ldm_start = max(sram_Crossbar_free_cycle, stage_ready_cycle, earliest_smsp_ready)
        ldm_done = ldm_start + t_sram_step
        sram_Crossbar_free_cycle = ldm_done

        # 3. Warps assigned to the 4 SMSPs execute HMMA.16816.F32 instructions
        #    In fused W1A16_F32, ALU unpack (shl.b32 + lop3.b32 + fma.rn.bf16x2) dual-issues
        #    on the INT32/FP16 pipes concurrently with HMMA on the Tensor Core pipe.
        mma_cycles_per_smsp = t_mma_step
        for s in range(gpu.smsp_per_sm):
            Operand_ready = ldm_start + (t_sram_step * ((s + 1) / gpu.smsp_per_sm))
            if Operand_ready > smsp_free_cycle[s]:
                stall = Operand_ready - smsp_free_cycle[s]
                total_smsp_stall_cycles += stall
                smsp_free_cycle[s] = Operand_ready
            smsp_free_cycle[s] += mma_cycles_per_smsp
            total_smsp_busy_cycles += mma_cycles_per_smsp

    total_wall_cycles = max(smsp_free_cycle)
    total_smsp_wall_slots = total_wall_cycles * gpu.smsp_per_sm
    tc_utilization_pct = (total_smsp_busy_cycles / total_smsp_wall_slots) * 100.0
    sass_wait_stall_pct = (total_smsp_stall_cycles / total_smsp_wall_slots) * 100.0
    achieved_tflops = gpu.peak_tflops(tau_cycles) * (tc_utilization_pct / 100.0)

    return {
        "t_mma_cycles": t_mma_step,
        "t_sram_cycles": t_sram_step,
        "compute_to_sram_ratio": t_mma_step / t_sram_step,
        "tc_util_pct": tc_utilization_pct,
        "sass_wait_pct": sass_wait_stall_pct,
        "achieved_tflops": achieved_tflops,
        "smem_kb": cfg.dynamic_smem_bytes / 1024.0,
    }


def evaluate_dram_ridge_points(gpu: GPUArchSpec, tau_cycles: int = 8) -> Dict[str, float]:
    peak_flops = gpu.peak_tflops(tau_cycles) * 1e12
    dram_bw = gpu.dram_bw_gbps * 1e9
    # Standard W16A16: 2 bytes per weight -> ridge point in FLOPs/Byte = Peak_FLOPs / DRAM_BW
    # Since each weight does 2*M FLOPs for 2 bytes read, FLOPs/Byte = M -> M_ridge_bf16 = Peak / DRAM_BW
    ridge_w16a16 = peak_flops / dram_bw
    # Fused Binary g128 (1.125 bits/weight = 14.222x compression):
    compression_ratio = 16.0 / 1.125
    effective_bw_tbps = (gpu.dram_bw_gbps * compression_ratio) / 1000.0
    ridge_w1a16 = ridge_w16a16 / compression_ratio
    return {
        "peak_tflops": gpu.peak_tflops(tau_cycles),
        "compression_ratio": compression_ratio,
        "effective_bw_tbps": effective_bw_tbps,
        "ridge_w16a16": ridge_w16a16,
        "ridge_w1a16": ridge_w1a16,
    }


# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Small-batch decode: wave quantization (2D tiling) vs Marlin-style Stream-K / Split-K
# Prior art: Frantar et al., MARLIN (arXiv:2408.11743). THIS IS AN ANALYTICAL MODEL WITH LABELED ASSUMPTIONS,
# NOT A MEASUREMENT. Its only job is to show which regime the 2D tile grid leaves SMs idle.
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

# ASSUMPTION: a decode GEMV is DRAM-bound and one SM can only pull a fraction of DRAM bandwidth; the GPU
# needs roughly this fraction of its SMs active to saturate DRAM. Real values vary by part and access pattern.
SM_FRACTION_TO_SATURATE_DRAM = 0.5


def decode_gemv_time_us(gpu: GPUArchSpec, m: int, n: int, k: int, bytes_per_weight: float,
                        active_sms: int, extra_dram_bytes: float = 0.0) -> float:
    """DRAM-bound time (us) to stream the weights once: bandwidth scales with active SMs up to saturation."""
    weight_bytes = n * k * bytes_per_weight
    saturating_sms = gpu.sm_count * SM_FRACTION_TO_SATURATE_DRAM
    bw_eff = gpu.dram_bw_gbps * 1e9 * min(1.0, active_sms / saturating_sms)
    return (weight_bytes + extra_dram_bytes) / bw_eff * 1e6


def compare_2d_tiling_vs_stream_k(gpu: GPUArchSpec, m: int, n: int, k: int,
                                  bytes_per_weight: float = 9.0 / 64.0,
                                  bm: int = 128, bn: int = 256) -> Dict[str, float]:
    ctas_2d = ((n + bn - 1) // bn) * ((m + bm - 1) // bm)
    active_2d = min(ctas_2d, gpu.sm_count)
    t_2d = decode_gemv_time_us(gpu, m, n, k, bytes_per_weight, active_2d)

    # Stream-K: BM = 16 (no wasted rows for M <= 16) and K is split so that every SM owns a contiguous slice
    n_tiles = (n + 63) // 64                      # BN = 64 per CTA in marlin_streamk_w1a16_decode_kernel
    splits = max(1, (gpu.sm_count * 2 + n_tiles - 1) // n_tiles)  # target 2 CTAs per SM
    ctas_sk = n_tiles * splits
    active_sk = min(ctas_sk, gpu.sm_count)
    # Split-K partials are accumulated by FP32 atomics in L2: model their cost as extra traffic of
    # M * N * 4 B * splits (an upper bound; it mostly stays in L2 rather than DRAM)
    t_sk = decode_gemv_time_us(gpu, m, n, k, bytes_per_weight, active_sk,
                               extra_dram_bytes=0.1 * m * n * 4.0 * splits)
    return {"ctas_2d": float(ctas_2d), "active_sms_2d": float(active_2d), "t_2d_us": t_2d,
            "ctas_sk": float(ctas_sk), "active_sms_sk": float(active_sk), "t_sk_us": t_sk,
            "speedup": t_2d / t_sk, "splits": float(splits)}


def simulate_hetero_cpu_gpu_roofline(
    weight_bytes: float,          # total compressed weight bytes streamed per token
    gpu_bw_gbps: float,           # effective GPU DRAM bandwidth
    cpu_bw_gbps: float,           # CPU DDR bandwidth available to the cold slice
    pcie_bw_gbps: float,          # effective PCIe bandwidth for activation dispatch / partial-sum merge
    vram_hot_bytes: float,        # VRAM budget that can hold hot weights
    m: int = 1, hidden: int = 4096, pcie_latency_us: float = 10.0,
) -> Dict[str, float]:
    """
    KTransformers / PowerInfer style split: a hot fraction f runs on the GPU, the rest on the CPU in parallel.
      t_gpu(f) = f * W / BW_gpu      t_cpu(f) = (1 - f) * W / BW_cpu + t_pcie
    The balanced split solves t_gpu = t_cpu; it is then capped by what fits in VRAM. Both slices are assumed
    purely bandwidth-bound, and the merge cost is one activation round trip (2 * M * D * 2 B + latency).
    """
    t_pcie = (2.0 * m * hidden * 2.0) / (pcie_bw_gbps * 1e9) + pcie_latency_us * 1e-6
    bg, bc = gpu_bw_gbps * 1e9, cpu_bw_gbps * 1e9
    f_bal = (weight_bytes / bc + t_pcie) / (weight_bytes / bg + weight_bytes / bc)
    f_bal = min(1.0, max(0.0, f_bal))
    f_cap = min(1.0, vram_hot_bytes / weight_bytes)
    f = min(f_bal, f_cap)
    t_gpu = f * weight_bytes / bg
    t_cpu = (1.0 - f) * weight_bytes / bc + t_pcie if f < 1.0 else 0.0
    return {"f_balanced": f_bal, "f_vram_cap": f_cap, "f_used": f,
            "t_gpu_ms": t_gpu * 1e3, "t_cpu_ms": t_cpu * 1e3, "t_token_ms": max(t_gpu, t_cpu) * 1e3,
            "t_pcie_us": t_pcie * 1e6, "bound_by": 0.0 if t_gpu >= t_cpu else 1.0}


def main() -> None:
    print("================================================================================")
    print("  WORKSTREAM 1: CYCLE-ACCURATE SMSP ROOFLINE & SCOREBOARD SIMULATOR")
    print("================================================================================\n")

    warp_tiles: List[TileConfig] = [
        TileConfig("Warp Tile 32x32 (Consumer Default)", 128, 64, 32, 32, 32, 4, 2, beta_conflict=1.18),
        TileConfig("Warp Tile 64x32 (Half-Rate cuBLAS)", 128, 128, 32, 64, 32, 4, 3, beta_conflict=1.12),
        TileConfig("Warp Tile 64x64 (Unlocked Optimal)", 128, 256, 32, 64, 64, 8, 4, beta_conflict=1.00),
        TileConfig("Warp Tile 128x64 (Max Reuse)",       256, 128, 32, 128, 64, 8, 4, beta_conflict=1.00),
        TileConfig("Warp Tile 64x64 Fused W1A16_F32",    128, 256, 32, 64, 64, 8, 4, beta_conflict=1.00, fused_w1a16=True),
    ]

    print("[1] Warp-Tile SRAM-to-Tensor-Core Roofline & Scoreboard Stall Analysis (AD102 RTX 4090):")
    print("-" * 114)
    print(f"{'Configuration':<35} | {'I_warp':>8} | {'State':<8} | {'T_MMA':>7} | {'T_SRAM':>7} | {'Ratio':>6} | {'SassWait':>8} | {'Achieved':>10}")
    print("-" * 114)

    for cfg in warp_tiles:
        for state_label, tau in [("Locked", 16), ("Unlocked", 8)]:
            res = simulate_smsp_cycle_accurate(AD102_RTX_4090, cfg, tau_cycles=tau)
            print(
                f"{cfg.label:<35} | {cfg.warp_arithmetic_intensity:>6.1f}   | {state_label:<8} | "
                f"{res['t_mma_cycles']:>7.1f} | {res['t_sram_cycles']:>7.1f} | "
                f"{res['compute_to_sram_ratio']:>5.2f}x | {res['sass_wait_pct']:>7.2f}% | "
                f"{res['achieved_tflops']:>7.1f} TF"
            )
    print("-" * 114)
    print()

    print("[2] Multi-Architecture Unlocked Roofline & Binary g128 (14.22x) DRAM Ridge Shift:")
    print("-" * 106)
    print(f"{'GPU Architecture':<22} | {'Phys BW':>10} | {'Eff W1 BW':>10} | {'Unlocked Peak':>14} | {'W16A16 Ridge':>14} | {'W1A16 Ridge':>14}")
    print("-" * 106)
    for gpu in [GA102_RTX_3090, GA102_RTX_3090_TI, AD102_RTX_4090]:
        r = evaluate_dram_ridge_points(gpu, tau_cycles=8)
        print(
            f"{gpu.name:<22} | {gpu.dram_bw_gbps:>7.1f} GB/s | {r['effective_bw_tbps']:>7.2f} TB/s | "
            f"{r['peak_tflops']:>10.1f} TF | M >= {r['ridge_w16a16']:>6.1f}   | M >= {r['ridge_w1a16']:>6.1f}"
        )
    print("-" * 106)
    print()

    print("[3] Small-batch decode, N = K = 4096, W1 (1.125 bpw): 2D tile grid vs Stream-K (ANALYTICAL MODEL, ASSUMPTION: "
          f"{int(SM_FRACTION_TO_SATURATE_DRAM * 100)}% of SMs saturate DRAM)")
    print("-" * 106)
    print(f"{'GPU':<20} | {'M':>3} | {'CTAs 2D':>7} | {'SMs 2D':>6} | {'t 2D us':>8} | {'CTAs SK':>7} | {'splits':>6} | {'t SK us':>8} | {'speedup':>7}")
    print("-" * 106)
    for gpu in [GA102_RTX_3090, AD102_RTX_4090]:
        for m in (1, 4, 8, 16, 32):
```
